# products/: the full table

Every scheme of this folder, against Sedoglavic's table of 2026-11-15.  "Own" is the gain beyond what that table
and the other schemes of this repository imply by sub-format, split and Kronecker product.  Every scheme is
stored with its dimensions in the order of "Format".  For a product, "Construction" is the base scheme and the
block sizes ⟨p×q×s⟩ (every entry of A becomes a p×q block, of B a q×s block, of C a p×s block), written so that
position by position they multiply to the format; the catalog file may hold the base scheme with its dimensions
in another order.  For a deletion it is the product scheme it starts from, made the same way, and the deleted
indices: in ⟨m×k×n⟩, `I5` removes row 5 of A (and of C), `J5` the inner index 5 and `K5` column 5 of B, counted
from 0.  "Invertible" lists the primes that occur in denominators.  See the README for the method and the
credits.

| Scheme | Format | Rank | Table | Gain | Own | Kind | Construction | Invertible | Exponent (table's) |
